[Worker-1] 生成的训练代码：

import os
import math
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
from torch_geometric.data import Data
from torch_geometric.nn import GATv2Conv
from torch_geometric.utils import coalesce
from scipy.sparse import csr_matrix
from sklearn.preprocessing import StandardScaler
from torch.optim.lr_scheduler import SequentialLR, LinearLR, CosineAnnealingLR
import warnings
warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')

# 设置随机种子保证可复现
SEED = 42
torch.manual_seed(SEED)
np.random.seed(SEED)
if torch.cuda.is_available():
    torch.cuda.manual_seed_all(SEED)
    torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = True

# ================= 1. 数据加载与预处理 =================
data_dir = '/data/coding/line3/dataset_b/cls_data/'
raw = np.load(os.path.join(data_dir, "B1.npz"))

# 严格按照文件原样使用邻接关系，不强制无向/对称/加自环
adj = csr_matrix((raw["adj_data"], raw["adj_indices"], raw["adj_indptr"]), shape=tuple(raw["adj_shape"]))
features = csr_matrix((raw["attr_data"], raw["attr_indices"], raw["attr_indptr"]), shape=tuple(raw["attr_shape"]))

adj_coo = adj.tocoo()
edge_index = torch.tensor(np.vstack([adj_coo.row, adj_coo.col]), dtype=torch.long)
edge_index = coalesce(edge_index)  # 仅去重，保持原始拓扑

# 特征预处理：仅 Z-score 标准化，彻底弃用 PCA
x_dense = features.toarray()
scaler = StandardScaler()
x_scaled = scaler.fit_transform(x_dense)
x = torch.tensor(x_scaled, dtype=torch.float32)

y = torch.tensor(raw["labels"], dtype=torch.long)
train_idx = raw["train_idx"]
test_idx = raw["test_idx"]

data = Data(x=x, edge_index=edge_index, y=y)

# ================= 2. 划分训练集/验证集 =================
def _reproduce_val_split(train_idx: np.ndarray) -> tuple:
    rng = np.random.RandomState(666)
    shuffled_idx = rng.permutation(train_idx)
    n_actual_train = math.ceil(len(shuffled_idx) * 0.8)
    actual_train_idx = shuffled_idx[:n_actual_train]
    val_idx = shuffled_idx[n_actual_train:]
    return actual_train_idx, val_idx

actual_train_idx, val_idx = _reproduce_val_split(train_idx)

# 设备与张量转换
device = torch.device('cuda' if torch.cuda.is_available() else 'cpu')
data = data.to(device)
train_idx_t = torch.tensor(actual_train_idx, dtype=torch.long, device=device)
val_idx_t = torch.tensor(val_idx, dtype=torch.long, device=device)
test_idx_t = torch.tensor(test_idx, dtype=torch.long, device=device)

# ================= 3. 模型定义 =================
class GATv2JKModel(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, in_channels, hidden_channels, out_channels, heads=4, dropout=0.25):
        super().__init__()
        self.dropout = dropout
        self.convs = nn.ModuleList()
        self.norms = nn.ModuleList()
        dim = hidden_channels * heads
        
        # 3层 GATv2，统一输出维度 dim
        self.convs.append(GATv2Conv(in_channels, hidden_channels, heads=heads, dropout=dropout, concat=True))
        self.norms.append(nn.LayerNorm(dim))
        self.convs.append(GATv2Conv(dim, hidden_channels, heads=heads, dropout=dropout, concat=True))
        self.norms.append(nn.LayerNorm(dim))
        self.convs.append(GATv2Conv(dim, hidden_channels, heads=heads, dropout=dropout, concat=True))
        self.norms.append(nn.LayerNorm(dim))
        
        self.classifier = nn.Linear(dim, out_channels)

    def forward(self, x, edge_index):
        xs = []
        for i, (conv, norm) in enumerate(zip(self.convs, self.norms)):
            x = conv(x, edge_index)
            x = norm(x)
            if i < len(self.convs) - 1:
                x = F.relu(x)
                x = F.dropout(x, p=self.dropout, training=self.training)
            xs.append(x)
        # JKNet (Max) 聚合多跳特征
        out = torch.stack(xs, dim=0).max(dim=0)[0]
        return self.classifier(out)

model = GATv2JKModel(in_channels=x.shape[1], hidden_channels=320, out_channels=8, heads=4, dropout=0.25).to(device)
optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(model.parameters(), lr=2e-4, weight_decay=8e-4)

# 10轮 Warmup + 余弦退火
epochs = 400
warmup_scheduler = LinearLR(optimizer, start_factor=0.1, end_factor=1.0, total_iters=10)
cosine_scheduler = CosineAnnealingLR(optimizer, T_max=epochs-10, eta_min=1e-6)
scheduler = SequentialLR(optimizer, schedulers=[warmup_scheduler, cosine_scheduler], milestones=[10])

criterion = nn.CrossEntropyLoss(label_smoothing=0.05)

# ================= 4. 训练配置 =================
save_path = './model_save_vb17/cls_model/worker_1/round_4'
os.makedirs(save_path, exist_ok=True)

def save_softmax_csv(idx, probs, filename, path):
    cols = ['test_idx'] + [f'class_{i}' for i in range(8)]
    df = pd.DataFrame(probs, columns=cols[1:])
    df.insert(0, 'test_idx', idx.astype(int))
    df.to_csv(os.path.join(path, filename), index=False, float_format='%.4f')

best_val_acc = 0.0
patience = 40
wait = 0

# ================= 5. 训练循环 =================
for epoch in range(1, epochs + 1):
    model.train()
    optimizer.zero_grad()
    out = model(data.x, data.edge_index)
    loss = criterion(out[train_idx_t], data.y[train_idx_t])
    loss.backward()
    optimizer.step()
    scheduler.step()

    if epoch % 10 == 0 or epoch == 1:
        model.eval()
        with torch.no_grad():
            out_eval = model(data.x, data.edge_index)
            val_pred = out_eval[val_idx_t].argmax(dim=1)
            val_acc = (val_pred == data.y[val_idx_t]).float().mean().item()

        print(f"Epoch {epoch:03d} | Val ACC: {val_acc:.4f} | Loss: {loss.item():.4f}")

        if val_acc > best_val_acc:
            best_val_acc = val_acc
            wait = 0
            torch.save(model.state_dict(), os.path.join(save_path, 'best_model.pth'))
            print(f"  -> New best model saved! Val ACC: {val_acc:.4f}")

            # 严格索引对齐保存验证集 Softmax
            val_probs = F.softmax(out_eval[val_idx_t], dim=1).cpu().numpy()
            save_softmax_csv(val_idx, val_probs, 'validation-softmax.csv', save_path)

            # 严格索引对齐保存测试集 Softmax (Eval模式)
            test_probs = F.softmax(out_eval[test_idx_t], dim=1).cpu().numpy()
            save_softmax_csv(test_idx, test_probs, 'B1-softmax.csv', save_path)

            # 保存 B1.csv
            test_pred = test_probs.argmax(axis=1)
            df_b1 = pd.DataFrame({'test_idx': test_idx.astype(int), 'label': test_pred})
            df_b1.to_csv(os.path.join(save_path, 'B1.csv'), index=False)
        else:
            wait += 1
            if wait >= patience:
                print(f"Early stopping at epoch {epoch}")
                break

# ================= 6. 测试集 TTA 增强 =================
print("Starting TTA for test set...")
model.load_state_dict(torch.load(os.path.join(save_path, 'best_model.pth'), map_location=device))
model.train()  # 开启 Dropout
tta_runs = 50
tta_probs = np.zeros((len(test_idx), 8))

with torch.no_grad():
    for i in range(tta_runs):
        out = model(data.x, data.edge_index)
        probs = F.softmax(out[test_idx_t], dim=1).cpu().numpy()
        tta_probs += probs
tta_probs /= tta_runs

# 覆盖最终输出文件
save_softmax_csv(test_idx, tta_probs, 'B1-softmax.csv', save_path)
final_pred = tta_probs.argmax(axis=1)
df_b1 = pd.DataFrame({'test_idx': test_idx.astype(int), 'label': final_pred})
df_b1.to_csv(os.path.join(save_path, 'B1.csv'), index=False)
print("TTA completed. Final B1.csv and B1-softmax.csv saved successfully.")

import os
import math
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
from torch_geometric.data import Data
from torch_geometric.nn import GATv2Conv
from torch_geometric.utils import add_self_loops, coalesce, to_undirected
from scipy.sparse import csr_matrix
from sklearn.preprocessing import StandardScaler
from sklearn.decomposition import PCA
from torch.optim.lr_scheduler import SequentialLR, LinearLR, CosineAnnealingLR
import warnings
warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')

# 设置随机种子保证可复现
torch.manual_seed(42)
np.random.seed(42)
if torch.cuda.is_available():
    torch.cuda.manual_seed_all(42)
    torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = True

# ================= 1. 数据加载与预处理 =================
data_dir = '/data/coding/line3/dataset_b/cls_data/'
raw = np.load(os.path.join(data_dir, "B1.npz"))

adj = csr_matrix((raw["adj_data"], raw["adj_indices"], raw["adj_indptr"]), shape=tuple(raw["adj_shape"]))
features = csr_matrix((raw["attr_data"], raw["attr_indices"], raw["attr_indptr"]), shape=tuple(raw["attr_shape"]))

# 构建 edge_index：强制对称归一化 + 自环 + 去重
adj_coo = adj.tocoo()
edge_index = torch.tensor(np.vstack([adj_coo.row, adj_coo.col]), dtype=torch.long)
edge_index = to_undirected(edge_index, num_nodes=adj.shape[0])
edge_index, _ = add_self_loops(edge_index, num_nodes=adj.shape[0])
edge_index = coalesce(edge_index)

# 特征预处理：Z-score + PCA(95%) 降噪提纯
x_dense = features.toarray()
scaler = StandardScaler()
x_scaled = scaler.fit_transform(x_dense)
pca = PCA(n_components=0.95, random_state=42)
x_pca = pca.fit_transform(x_scaled)
x = torch.tensor(x_pca, dtype=torch.float32)

y = torch.tensor(raw["labels"], dtype=torch.long)
train_idx = raw["train_idx"]
test_idx = raw["test_idx"]

data = Data(x=x, edge_index=edge_index, y=y)

# ================= 2. 划分训练集/验证集 =================
def _reproduce_val_split(train_idx: np.ndarray) -> tuple:
    rng = np.random.RandomState(666)
    shuffled_idx = rng.permutation(train_idx)
    n_actual_train = math.ceil(len(shuffled_idx) * 0.8)
    actual_train_idx = shuffled_idx[:n_actual_train]
    val_idx = shuffled_idx[n_actual_train:]
    return actual_train_idx, val_idx

actual_train_idx, val_idx = _reproduce_val_split(train_idx)

# 设备与张量转换
device = torch.device('cuda' if torch.cuda.is_available() else 'cpu')
data = data.to(device)
train_idx_t = torch.tensor(actual_train_idx, dtype=torch.long, device=device)
val_idx_t = torch.tensor(val_idx, dtype=torch.long, device=device)
test_idx_t = torch.tensor(test_idx, dtype=torch.long, device=device)

# ================= 3. 模型定义 =================
class GATv2JKModel(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, in_channels, hidden_channels, out_channels, heads=4, dropout=0.15):
        super().__init__()
        self.dropout = dropout
        self.convs = nn.ModuleList()
        self.norms = nn.ModuleList()
        dim = hidden_channels * heads
        
        # 3层 GATv2
        self.convs.append(GATv2Conv(in_channels, hidden_channels, heads=heads, dropout=dropout, concat=True))
        self.norms.append(nn.LayerNorm(dim))
        self.convs.append(GATv2Conv(dim, hidden_channels, heads=heads, dropout=dropout, concat=True))
        self.norms.append(nn.LayerNorm(dim))
        self.convs.append(GATv2Conv(dim, hidden_channels, heads=heads, dropout=dropout, concat=True))
        self.norms.append(nn.LayerNorm(dim))
        
        self.classifier = nn.Linear(dim, out_channels)

    def forward(self, x, edge_index):
        xs = []
        for i, (conv, norm) in enumerate(zip(self.convs, self.norms)):
            x = conv(x, edge_index)
            x = norm(x)
            if i < len(self.convs) - 1:
                x = F.relu(x)
                x = F.dropout(x, p=self.dropout, training=self.training)
            xs.append(x)
        # JKNet (Max) 聚合多跳特征
        out = torch.stack(xs, dim=0).max(dim=0)[0]
        return self.classifier(out)

model = GATv2JKModel(in_channels=x.shape[1], hidden_channels=256, out_channels=8, heads=4, dropout=0.15).to(device)
optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(model.parameters(), lr=2e-4, weight_decay=1e-3)

# 5% Warmup (20 epochs) + 余弦退火
epochs = 400
warmup_iters = int(epochs * 0.05)
warmup_scheduler = LinearLR(optimizer, start_factor=0.1, end_factor=1.0, total_iters=warmup_iters)
cosine_scheduler = CosineAnnealingLR(optimizer, T_max=epochs-warmup_iters, eta_min=1e-6)
scheduler = SequentialLR(optimizer, schedulers=[warmup_scheduler, cosine_scheduler], milestones=[warmup_iters])

criterion = nn.CrossEntropyLoss(label_smoothing=0.1)

# ================= 4. 训练配置 =================
save_path = './model_save_vb17/cls_model/worker_2/round_4'
os.makedirs(save_path, exist_ok=True)

def save_softmax_csv(idx, probs, filename, path):
    cols = ['test_idx'] + [f'class_{i}' for i in range(8)]
    df = pd.DataFrame(probs, columns=cols[1:])
    df.insert(0, 'test_idx', idx.astype(int))
    df.to_csv(os.path.join(path, filename), index=False, float_format='%.4f')

best_val_acc = 0.0
patience = 40
wait = 0

# ================= 5. 训练循环 =================
for epoch in range(1, epochs + 1):
    model.train()
    optimizer.zero_grad()
    out = model(data.x, data.edge_index)
    loss = criterion(out[train_idx_t], data.y[train_idx_t])
    loss.backward()
    optimizer.step()
    scheduler.step()

    # 验证集评估
    model.eval()
    with torch.no_grad():
        out_eval = model(data.x, data.edge_index)
        val_pred = out_eval[val_idx_t].argmax(dim=1)
        val_acc = (val_pred == data.y[val_idx_t]).float().mean().item()

    if epoch % 10 == 0 or epoch == 1:
        print(f"Epoch {epoch:03d} | Val ACC: {val_acc:.4f} | Loss: {loss.item():.4f}")

    if val_acc > best_val_acc:
        best_val_acc = val_acc
        wait = 0
        torch.save(model.state_dict(), os.path.join(save_path, 'best_model.pth'))
        if epoch % 10 == 0 or epoch == 1:
            print(f"  -> New best model saved! Val ACC: {val_acc:.4f}")

        # 严格索引对齐保存验证集 Softmax
        val_probs = F.softmax(out_eval[val_idx], dim=1).cpu().numpy()
        save_softmax_csv(val_idx, val_probs, 'validation-softmax.csv', save_path)

        # 严格索引对齐保存测试集 Softmax (Eval模式)
        test_probs = F.softmax(out_eval[test_idx], dim=1).cpu().numpy()
        save_softmax_csv(test_idx, test_probs, 'B1-softmax.csv', save_path)

        # 保存 B1.csv
        test_pred = test_probs.argmax(axis=1)
        df_b1 = pd.DataFrame({'test_idx': test_idx.astype(int), 'label': test_pred})
        df_b1.to_csv(os.path.join(save_path, 'B1.csv'), index=False)
    else:
        wait += 1
        if wait >= patience:
            print(f"Early stopping at epoch {epoch}")
            break

# ================= 6. 测试集 TTA 增强 =================
print("Starting TTA for test set...")
model.load_state_dict(torch.load(os.path.join(save_path, 'best_model.pth'), map_location=device))
model.train()  # 开启 Dropout
tta_runs = 50
tta_probs = np.zeros((len(test_idx), 8))

with torch.no_grad():
    for i in range(tta_runs):
        out = model(data.x, data.edge_index)
        probs = F.softmax(out[test_idx], dim=1).cpu().numpy()
        tta_probs += probs
tta_probs /= tta_runs

# 覆盖最终输出文件
save_softmax_csv(test_idx, tta_probs, 'B1-softmax.csv', save_path)
final_pred = tta_probs.argmax(axis=1)
df_b1 = pd.DataFrame({'test_idx': test_idx.astype(int), 'label': final_pred})
df_b1.to_csv(os.path.join(save_path, 'B1.csv'), index=False)
print("TTA completed. Final B1.csv and B1-softmax.csv saved successfully.")

[Worker-1] 生成的优化方向为: 瓶颈定位：同质率仅0.32，GATv2邻居聚合受异质性抑制，导致ACC卡在0.53；多轮Loss持续下降但Val ACC plateau，属轻度过拟合与表征饱和；Z-score/PCA与多重正则（Dropout+LS+TTA）叠加压制了注意力有效信号。

调优方案：
1. 架构异质适配：回溯Round9基线，引入Ego-Neighbor解耦（自节点与邻居独立投影后拼接），保留3层GATv2+4头，JK改用Concat，叠加残差直连，LayerNorm后置。
2. 预处理与拓扑：弃用Z-score/PCA，改用原始密集特征+L2行归一化（稳定异质图注意力权重）；强制对称归一化+自环，边无向化，不扰动拓扑。
3. 正则精简：仅保留Dropout=0.15，移除Label Smoothing、DropPath及拓扑TTA；推理仅用10次Dropout TTA概率平均。
4. 超参：lr=2e-4，weight_decay=1e-4，5%Warmup+Cosine退火；标准CE损失（禁类别加权防ACC回落）；Epochs=400，EarlyStopping patience=30。全图训练，预期突破0.55。

落地调优方案：
1. 架构适配异质性：保留3层GATv2，改为**Ego-Neighbor分离架构**，每层将中心节点特征与聚合邻居特征Concat后再过线性层，缓解异质平滑。

5. 推理与损失：标准CE损失(遵循指南暂不加类权重)；TTA降至20次(Dropout=0.1)防概率过平滑。此配置聚焦异质解耦与容量释放，预期Val ACC突破0.55。

[Worker-1] 生成的训练代码：

import os
import math
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
from torch_geometric.data import Data
from torch_geometric.nn import GATv2Conv
from torch_geometric.utils import add_self_loops, coalesce, to_undirected
from scipy.sparse import csr_matrix
from sklearn.preprocessing import normalize
from torch.optim.lr_scheduler import SequentialLR, LinearLR, CosineAnnealingLR
import warnings
warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')

# 设置随机种子保证可复现
torch.manual_seed(42)
np.random.seed(42)
if torch.cuda.is_available():
    torch.cuda.manual_seed_all(42)
    torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = True

# ================= 1. 数据加载与预处理 =================
data_dir = '/data/coding/line3/dataset_b/cls_data/'
raw = np.load(os.path.join(data_dir, "B1.npz"))

adj = csr_matrix((raw["adj_data"], raw["adj_indices"], raw["adj_indptr"]), shape=tuple(raw["adj_shape"]))
features = csr_matrix((raw["attr_data"], raw["attr_indices"], raw["attr_indptr"]), shape=tuple(raw["attr_shape"]))

# 构建 edge_index：强制对称归一化 + 自环 + 去重
adj_coo = adj.tocoo()
edge_index = torch.tensor(np.vstack([adj_coo.row, adj_coo.col]), dtype=torch.long)
edge_index = to_undirected(edge_index, num_nodes=adj.shape[0])
edge_index, _ = add_self_loops(edge_index, num_nodes=adj.shape[0])
edge_index = coalesce(edge_index)

# 特征预处理：弃用Z-score/PCA，改用原始密集特征+L2行归一化
x_dense = features.toarray()
x_l2 = normalize(x_dense, norm='l2', axis=1)
x = torch.tensor(x_l2, dtype=torch.float32)

y = torch.tensor(raw["labels"], dtype=torch.long)
train_idx = raw["train_idx"]
test_idx = raw["test_idx"]

data = Data(x=x, edge_index=edge_index, y=y)

# ================= 2. 划分训练集/验证集 =================
def _reproduce_val_split(train_idx: np.ndarray) -> tuple:
    rng = np.random.RandomState(666)
    shuffled_idx = rng.permutation(train_idx)
    n_actual_train = math.ceil(len(shuffled_idx) * 0.8)
    actual_train_idx = shuffled_idx[:n_actual_train]
    val_idx = shuffled_idx[n_actual_train:]
    return actual_train_idx, val_idx

actual_train_idx, val_idx = _reproduce_val_split(train_idx)

# 设备与张量转换
device = torch.device('cuda' if torch.cuda.is_available() else 'cpu')
data = data.to(device)
train_idx_t = torch.tensor(actual_train_idx, dtype=torch.long, device=device)
val_idx_t = torch.tensor(val_idx, dtype=torch.long, device=device)
test_idx_t = torch.tensor(test_idx, dtype=torch.long, device=device)

# ================= 3. 模型定义 =================
class GATv2JKModel(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, in_channels, hidden_channels, out_channels, heads=4, dropout=0.15):
        super().__init__()
        self.dropout = dropout
        self.convs = nn.ModuleList()
        self.norms = nn.ModuleList()
        dim = hidden_channels * heads
        
        # 3层 GATv2，统一输出维度 dim
        self.convs.append(GATv2Conv(in_channels, hidden_channels, heads=heads, dropout=dropout, concat=True))
        self.norms.append(nn.LayerNorm(dim))
        self.convs.append(GATv2Conv(dim, hidden_channels, heads=heads, dropout=dropout, concat=True))
        self.norms.append(nn.LayerNorm(dim))
        self.convs.append(GATv2Conv(dim, hidden_channels, heads=heads, dropout=dropout, concat=True))
        self.norms.append(nn.LayerNorm(dim))
        
        # JK Concat 聚合多跳特征
        self.classifier = nn.Linear(dim * 3, out_channels)

    def forward(self, x, edge_index):
        xs = []
        for i, (conv, norm) in enumerate(zip(self.convs, self.norms)):
            x = conv(x, edge_index)
            x = norm(x)
            if i < len(self.convs) - 1:
                x = F.relu(x)
                x = F.dropout(x, p=self.dropout, training=self.training)
            xs.append(x)
        out = torch.cat(xs, dim=1)
        return self.classifier(out)

model = GATv2JKModel(in_channels=x.shape[1], hidden_channels=256, out_channels=8, heads=4, dropout=0.15).to(device)
optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(model.parameters(), lr=2e-4, weight_decay=1e-4)

# 5% Warmup + 余弦退火
epochs = 400
warmup_iters = int(epochs * 0.05)
warmup_scheduler = LinearLR(optimizer, start_factor=0.1, end_factor=1.0, total_iters=warmup_iters)
cosine_scheduler = CosineAnnealingLR(optimizer, T_max=epochs-warmup_iters, eta_min=1e-6)
scheduler = SequentialLR(optimizer, schedulers=[warmup_scheduler, cosine_scheduler], milestones=[warmup_iters])

criterion = nn.CrossEntropyLoss() # 标准CE损失，移除Label Smoothing

# ================= 4. 训练配置 =================
save_path = './model_save_vb17/cls_model/worker_1/round_5'
os.makedirs(save_path, exist_ok=True)

def save_softmax_csv(idx, probs, filename, path):
    cols = ['test_idx'] + [f'class_{i}' for i in range(8)]
    df = pd.DataFrame(probs, columns=cols[1:])
    df.insert(0, 'test_idx', idx.astype(int))
    df.to_csv(os.path.join(path, filename), index=False, float_format='%.4f')

best_val_acc = 0.0
patience = 40
wait = 0

# ================= 5. 训练循环 =================
for epoch in range(1, epochs + 1):
    model.train()
    optimizer.zero_grad()
    out = model(data.x, data.edge_index)
    loss = criterion(out[train_idx_t], data.y[train_idx_t])
    loss.backward()
    optimizer.step()
    scheduler.step()

    if epoch % 10 == 0 or epoch == 1:
        model.eval()
        with torch.no_grad():
            out_eval = model(data.x, data.edge_index)
            val_pred = out_eval[val_idx_t].argmax(dim=1)
            val_acc = (val_pred == data.y[val_idx_t]).float().mean().item()

        print(f"Epoch {epoch:03d} | Val ACC: {val_acc:.4f} | Loss: {loss.item():.4f}")

        if val_acc > best_val_acc:
            best_val_acc = val_acc
            wait = 0
            torch.save(model.state_dict(), os.path.join(save_path, 'best_model.pth'))
            print(f"  -> New best model saved! Val ACC: {val_acc:.4f}")

            # 严格索引对齐保存验证集 Softmax
            val_probs = F.softmax(out_eval[val_idx_t], dim=1).cpu().numpy()
            save_softmax_csv(val_idx, val_probs, 'validation-softmax.csv', save_path)

            # 严格索引对齐保存测试集 Softmax (Eval模式)
            test_probs = F.softmax(out_eval[test_idx_t], dim=1).cpu().numpy()
            save_softmax_csv(test_idx, test_probs, 'B1-softmax.csv', save_path)

            # 保存 B1.csv
            test_pred = test_probs.argmax(axis=1)
            df_b1 = pd.DataFrame({'test_idx': test_idx.astype(int), 'label': test_pred})
            df_b1.to_csv(os.path.join(save_path, 'B1.csv'), index=False)
        else:
            wait += 1
            if wait >= patience:
                print(f"Early stopping at epoch {epoch}")
                break

# ================= 6. 测试集 TTA 增强 =================
print("Starting TTA for test set...")
model.load_state_dict(torch.load(os.path.join(save_path, 'best_model.pth'), map_location=device))
model.train()  # 开启 Dropout 进行 TTA
tta_runs = 50
tta_probs = np.zeros((len(test_idx), 8))

with torch.no_grad():
    for i in range(tta_runs):
        out = model(data.x, data.edge_index)
        probs = F.softmax(out[test_idx_t], dim=1).cpu().numpy()
        tta_probs += probs
tta_probs /= tta_runs

# 覆盖最终输出文件
save_softmax_csv(test_idx, tta_probs, 'B1-softmax.csv', save_path)
final_pred = tta_probs.argmax(axis=1)
df_b1 = pd.DataFrame({'test_idx': test_idx.astype(int), 'label': final_pred})
df_b1.to_csv(os.path.join(save_path, 'B1.csv'), index=False)
print("TTA completed. Final B1.csv and B1-softmax.csv saved successfully.")

[Worker-0] 生成的训练代码：

import os
import math
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
from torch_geometric.data import Data
from torch_geometric.nn import GATv2Conv
from torch_geometric.utils import add_self_loops, coalesce, to_undirected
from scipy.sparse import csr_matrix
from sklearn.preprocessing import StandardScaler
from torch.optim.lr_scheduler import SequentialLR, LinearLR, CosineAnnealingLR
import warnings
warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')

# 设置随机种子保证可复现
torch.manual_seed(42)
np.random.seed(42)
if torch.cuda.is_available():
    torch.cuda.manual_seed_all(42)
    torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = True

# ================= 1. 数据加载与预处理 =================
data_dir = '/data/coding/line3/dataset_b/cls_data/'
raw = np.load(os.path.join(data_dir, "B1.npz"))

adj = csr_matrix((raw["adj_data"], raw["adj_indices"], raw["adj_indptr"]), shape=tuple(raw["adj_shape"]))
features = csr_matrix((raw["attr_data"], raw["attr_indices"], raw["attr_indptr"]), shape=tuple(raw["attr_shape"]))

# 构建 edge_index：强制对称归一化 + 自环 + 去重
adj_coo = adj.tocoo()
edge_index = torch.tensor(np.vstack([adj_coo.row, adj_coo.col]), dtype=torch.long)
edge_index = to_undirected(edge_index, num_nodes=adj.shape[0])
edge_index, _ = add_self_loops(edge_index, num_nodes=adj.shape[0])
edge_index = coalesce(edge_index)

# 特征预处理：仅 Z-score 标准化，关闭 PCA 保真
x_dense = features.toarray()
scaler = StandardScaler()
x_scaled = scaler.fit_transform(x_dense)
x = torch.tensor(x_scaled, dtype=torch.float32)

y = torch.tensor(raw["labels"], dtype=torch.long)
train_idx = raw["train_idx"]
test_idx = raw["test_idx"]

data = Data(x=x, edge_index=edge_index, y=y)

# ================= 2. 划分训练集/验证集 =================
def _reproduce_val_split(train_idx: np.ndarray) -> tuple:
    rng = np.random.RandomState(666)
    shuffled_idx = rng.permutation(train_idx)
    n_actual_train = math.ceil(len(shuffled_idx) * 0.8)
    actual_train_idx = shuffled_idx[:n_actual_train]
    val_idx = shuffled_idx[n_actual_train:]
    return actual_train_idx, val_idx

actual_train_idx, val_idx = _reproduce_val_split(train_idx)

# 设备与张量转换
device = torch.device('cuda' if torch.cuda.is_available() else 'cpu')
data = data.to(device)
train_idx_t = torch.tensor(actual_train_idx, dtype=torch.long, device=device)
val_idx_t = torch.tensor(val_idx, dtype=torch.long, device=device)
test_idx_t = torch.tensor(test_idx, dtype=torch.long, device=device)

# ================= 3. 模型定义 =================
class GATv2JKModel(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, in_channels, hidden_channels, out_channels, heads=4, dropout=0.25):
        super().__init__()
        self.dropout = dropout
        self.convs = nn.ModuleList()
        self.norms = nn.ModuleList()
        dim = hidden_channels * heads
        
        # 3层 GATv2，统一输出维度 dim
        self.convs.append(GATv2Conv(in_channels, hidden_channels, heads=heads, dropout=dropout, concat=True))
        self.norms.append(nn.LayerNorm(dim))
        self.convs.append(GATv2Conv(dim, hidden_channels, heads=heads, dropout=dropout, concat=True))
        self.norms.append(nn.LayerNorm(dim))
        self.convs.append(GATv2Conv(dim, hidden_channels, heads=heads, dropout=dropout, concat=True))
        self.norms.append(nn.LayerNorm(dim))
        
        self.classifier = nn.Linear(dim, out_channels)

    def forward(self, x, edge_index):
        xs = []
        for i, (conv, norm) in enumerate(zip(self.convs, self.norms)):
            x = conv(x, edge_index)
            x = norm(x)
            if i < len(self.convs) - 1:
                x = F.gelu(x)
                x = F.dropout(x, p=self.dropout, training=self.training)
            xs.append(x)
        # JKNet (Max) 聚合多跳特征
        out = torch.stack(xs, dim=0).max(dim=0)[0]
        return self.classifier(out)

# 降低 hidden_channels 至 64 配合 heads=4 保证显存安全 (<20GB)
model = GATv2JKModel(in_channels=x.shape[1], hidden_channels=64, out_channels=8, heads=4, dropout=0.25).to(device)
optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(model.parameters(), lr=3e-4, weight_decay=6e-4)

# 10轮 Warmup + 余弦退火
epochs = 400
warmup_scheduler = LinearLR(optimizer, start_factor=0.1, end_factor=1.0, total_iters=10)
cosine_scheduler = CosineAnnealingLR(optimizer, T_max=epochs-10, eta_min=1e-6)
scheduler = SequentialLR(optimizer, schedulers=[warmup_scheduler, cosine_scheduler], milestones=[10])

criterion = nn.CrossEntropyLoss(label_smoothing=0.05)

# ================= 4. 训练配置 =================
save_path = './model_save_vb17/cls_model/worker_0/round_3'
os.makedirs(save_path, exist_ok=True)

def save_softmax_csv(idx, probs, filename, path):
    cols = ['test_idx'] + [f'class_{i}' for i in range(8)]
    df = pd.DataFrame(probs, columns=cols[1:])
    df.insert(0, 'test_idx', idx.astype(int))
    df.to_csv(os.path.join(path, filename), index=False, float_format='%.4f')

best_val_acc = 0.0
patience = 40
wait = 0

# 清理潜在显存碎片
if torch.cuda.is_available():
    torch.cuda.empty_cache()

# ================= 5. 训练循环 =================
for epoch in range(1, epochs + 1):
    model.train()
    optimizer.zero_grad()
    out = model(data.x, data.edge_index)
    loss = criterion(out[train_idx_t], data.y[train_idx_t])
    loss.backward()
    optimizer.step()
    scheduler.step()

    if epoch % 10 == 0 or epoch == 1:
        model.eval()
        with torch.no_grad():
            out_eval = model(data.x, data.edge_index)
            val_pred = out_eval[val_idx_t].argmax(dim=1)
            val_acc = (val_pred == data.y[val_idx_t]).float().mean().item()

        print(f"Epoch {epoch:03d} | Val ACC: {val_acc:.4f} | Loss: {loss.item():.4f}")

        if val_acc > best_val_acc:
            best_val_acc = val_acc
            wait = 0
            torch.save(model.state_dict(), os.path.join(save_path, 'best_model.pth'))
            print(f"  -> New best model saved! Val ACC: {val_acc:.4f}")

            # 严格索引对齐保存验证集 Softmax
            val_probs = F.softmax(out_eval[val_idx_t], dim=1).cpu().numpy()
            save_softmax_csv(val_idx, val_probs, 'validation-softmax.csv', save_path)

            # 严格索引对齐保存测试集 Softmax (Eval模式)
            test_probs = F.softmax(out_eval[test_idx_t], dim=1).cpu().numpy()
            save_softmax_csv(test_idx, test_probs, 'B1-softmax.csv', save_path)

            # 保存 B1.csv
            test_pred = test_probs.argmax(axis=1)
            df_b1 = pd.DataFrame({'test_idx': test_idx.astype(int), 'label': test_pred})
            df_b1.to_csv(os.path.join(save_path, 'B1.csv'), index=False)
        else:
            wait += 1
            if wait >= patience:
                print(f"Early stopping at epoch {epoch}")
                break

# ================= 6. 测试集 TTA 增强 =================
print("Starting TTA for test set...")
model.load_state_dict(torch.load(os.path.join(save_path, 'best_model.pth'), map_location=device))
model.train()  # 开启 Dropout
tta_runs = 50
tta_probs = np.zeros((len(test_idx), 8))

with torch.no_grad():
    for _ in range(tta_runs):
        out = model(data.x, data.edge_index)
        probs = F.softmax(out[test_idx_t], dim=1).cpu().numpy()
        tta_probs += probs
tta_probs /= tta_runs

# 覆盖最终输出文件
save_softmax_csv(test_idx, tta_probs, 'B1-softmax.csv', save_path)
final_pred = tta_probs.argmax(axis=1)
df_b1 = pd.DataFrame({'test_idx': test_idx.astype(int), 'label': final_pred})
df_b1.to_csv(os.path.join(save_path, 'B1.csv'), index=False)
print("TTA completed. Final B1.csv and B1-softmax.csv saved successfully.")

[Worker-1] 🔥 执行冒烟测试...
[Worker-2] 生成的训练代码：

import os
import math
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
from torch_geometric.data import Data
from torch_geometric.nn import GATv2Conv
from torch_geometric.utils import coalesce
from scipy.sparse import csr_matrix
from sklearn.preprocessing import StandardScaler
from torch.optim.lr_scheduler import SequentialLR, LinearLR, CosineAnnealingLR
import warnings
warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')

# 设置随机种子保证可复现
torch.manual_seed(42)
np.random.seed(42)
if torch.cuda.is_available():
    torch.cuda.manual_seed_all(42)
    torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = True

# ================= 1. 数据加载与预处理 =================
data_dir = '/data/coding/line3/dataset_b/cls_data/'
raw = np.load(os.path.join(data_dir, "B1.npz"))

adj = csr_matrix((raw["adj_data"], raw["adj_indices"], raw["adj_indptr"]), shape=tuple(raw["adj_shape"]))
features = csr_matrix((raw["attr_data"], raw["attr_indices"], raw["attr_indptr"]), shape=tuple(raw["attr_shape"]))

# 构建 edge_index：严格按原文件邻接关系，不假设无向/对称/自环，仅去重
adj_coo = adj.tocoo()
edge_index = torch.tensor(np.vstack([adj_coo.row, adj_coo.col]), dtype=torch.long)
edge_index = coalesce(edge_index)

# 特征预处理：仅 Z-score 标准化，全量保留原始特征
x_dense = features.toarray()
scaler = StandardScaler()
x_scaled = scaler.fit_transform(x_dense)
x = torch.tensor(x_scaled, dtype=torch.float32)

y = torch.tensor(raw["labels"], dtype=torch.long)
train_idx = raw["train_idx"]
test_idx = raw["test_idx"]

data = Data(x=x, edge_index=edge_index, y=y)

# ================= 2. 划分训练集/验证集 =================
def _reproduce_val_split(train_idx: np.ndarray) -> tuple:
    rng = np.random.RandomState(666)
    shuffled_idx = rng.permutation(train_idx)
    n_actual_train = math.ceil(len(shuffled_idx) * 0.8)
    actual_train_idx = shuffled_idx[:n_actual_train]
    val_idx = shuffled_idx[n_actual_train:]
    return actual_train_idx, val_idx

actual_train_idx, val_idx = _reproduce_val_split(train_idx)

device = torch.device('cuda' if torch.cuda.is_available() else 'cpu')
data = data.to(device)
train_idx_t = torch.tensor(actual_train_idx, dtype=torch.long, device=device)
val_idx_t = torch.tensor(val_idx, dtype=torch.long, device=device)
test_idx_t = torch.tensor(test_idx, dtype=torch.long, device=device)

# ================= 3. 模型定义 (Ego-Neighbor分离 + JK-Concat) =================
class EgoNeighborGATv2Layer(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, in_channels, out_channels, heads=4, dropout=0.15):
        super().__init__()
        self.conv = GATv2Conv(in_channels, out_channels, heads=heads, dropout=dropout, concat=True, add_self_loops=False)
        self.lin = nn.Linear(in_channels + out_channels * heads, out_channels * heads)
        self.norm = nn.LayerNorm(out_channels * heads)
        self.dropout = dropout

    def forward(self, x, edge_index):
        out = self.conv(x, edge_index)
        x = torch.cat([x, out], dim=1)
        x = self.lin(x)
        x = self.norm(x)
        x = F.relu(x)
        return F.dropout(x, p=self.dropout, training=self.training)

class GATv2EgoJKModel(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, in_channels, hidden_channels, out_channels, heads=4, dropout=0.15):
        super().__init__()
        self.convs = nn.ModuleList()
        dim = hidden_channels * heads
        
        self.convs.append(EgoNeighborGATv2Layer(in_channels, hidden_channels, heads, dropout))
        self.convs.append(EgoNeighborGATv2Layer(dim, hidden_channels, heads, dropout))
        self.convs.append(EgoNeighborGATv2Layer(dim, hidden_channels, heads, dropout))
        
        self.classifier = nn.Linear(dim * 3, out_channels)

    def forward(self, x, edge_index):
        xs = []
        for conv in self.convs:
            x = conv(x, edge_index)
            xs.append(x)
        out = torch.cat(xs, dim=1)
        return self.classifier(out)

model = GATv2EgoJKModel(in_channels=x.shape[1], hidden_channels=320, out_channels=8, heads=4, dropout=0.15).to(device)
optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(model.parameters(), lr=2e-4, weight_decay=3e-4)

# 10轮 Warmup + 余弦退火
epochs = 400
warmup_scheduler = LinearLR(optimizer, start_factor=0.1, end_factor=1.0, total_iters=10)
cosine_scheduler = CosineAnnealingLR(optimizer, T_max=epochs-10, eta_min=1e-6)
scheduler = SequentialLR(optimizer, schedulers=[warmup_scheduler, cosine_scheduler], milestones=[10])

criterion = nn.CrossEntropyLoss()

# ================= 4. 训练配置 =================
save_path = './model_save_vb17/cls_model/worker_2/round_5'
os.makedirs(save_path, exist_ok=True)

def save_softmax_csv(idx, probs, filename, path):
    cols = ['test_idx'] + [f'class_{i}' for i in range(8)]
    df = pd.DataFrame(probs, columns=cols[1:])
    df.insert(0, 'test_idx', idx.astype(int))
    df.to_csv(os.path.join(path, filename), index=False, float_format='%.4f')

best_val_acc = 0.0
patience = 30
wait = 0

# ================= 5. 训练循环 =================
for epoch in range(1, epochs + 1):
    model.train()
    optimizer.zero_grad()
    out = model(data.x, data.edge_index)
    loss = criterion(out[train_idx_t], data.y[train_idx_t])
    loss.backward()
    optimizer.step()
    scheduler.step()

    if epoch % 10 == 0 or epoch == 1:
        model.eval()
        with torch.no_grad():
            out_eval = model(data.x, data.edge_index)
            val_pred = out_eval[val_idx_t].argmax(dim=1)
            val_acc = (val_pred == data.y[val_idx_t]).float().mean().item()

        print(f"Epoch {epoch:03d} | Val ACC: {val_acc:.4f} | Loss: {loss.item():.4f}")

        if val_acc > best_val_acc:
            best_val_acc = val_acc
            wait = 0
            torch.save(model.state_dict(), os.path.join(save_path, 'best_model.pth'))
            print(f"  -> New best model saved! Val ACC: {val_acc:.4f}")

            # 严格索引对齐保存验证集 Softmax
            val_probs = F.softmax(out_eval[val_idx], dim=1).cpu().numpy()
            save_softmax_csv(val_idx, val_probs, 'validation-softmax.csv', save_path)

            # 严格索引对齐保存测试集 Softmax (Eval模式)
            test_probs = F.softmax(out_eval[test_idx], dim=1).cpu().numpy()
            save_softmax_csv(test_idx, test_probs, 'B1-softmax.csv', save_path)

            # 保存 B1.csv
            test_pred = test_probs.argmax(axis=1)
            df_b1 = pd.DataFrame({'test_idx': test_idx.astype(int), 'label': test_pred})
            df_b1.to_csv(os.path.join(save_path, 'B1.csv'), index=False)
        else:
            wait += 1
            if wait >= patience:
                print(f"Early stopping at epoch {epoch}")
                break

# ================= 6. 测试集 TTA 增强 =================
print("Starting TTA for test set...")
model.load_state_dict(torch.load(os.path.join(save_path, 'best_model.pth'), map_location=device))
model.train()  # 开启 Dropout
tta_runs = 40
tta_probs = np.zeros((len(test_idx), 8))

with torch.no_grad():
    for i in range(tta_runs):
        out = model(data.x, data.edge_index)
        probs = F.softmax(out[test_idx], dim=1).cpu().numpy()
        tta_probs += probs
tta_probs /= tta_runs

# 覆盖最终输出文件
save_softmax_csv(test_idx, tta_probs, 'B1-softmax.csv', save_path)
final_pred = tta_probs.argmax(axis=1)
df_b1 = pd.DataFrame({'test_idx': test_idx.astype(int), 'label': final_pred})
df_b1.to_csv(os.path.join(save_path, 'B1.csv'), index=False)
print("TTA completed. Final B1.csv and B1-softmax.csv saved successfully.")

[Worker-1] 生成的优化方向为: 瓶颈定位：同质率0.32属强异质图，GATv2直接邻居聚合引发特征冲突；JK-Max过滤过度；Hidden=256容量见顶；50次TTA叠加Dropout致推理噪声大，Loss降而ACC卡死0.53。

调优方案：
1.架构异质化：3层GATv2引入H2GCN核心思想，强制Ego-Neighbor表征分离拼接，JK改Concat保留多跳尺度。
2.容量与归一化：Hidden升至384，全层LayerNorm稳深层梯度。
3.正则精简：Dropout=0.2，DropPath=0.05，严禁DropEdge；验证集关闭TTA单步评估，测试仅10次Dropout-TTA。
4.预处理与超参：仅Z-score，弃PCA；lr=2e-4，wd=5e-4，5%Warmup+余弦退火；CE+LabelSmoothing(0.05)，Epoch=400，Patience=40。直击异质表征瓶颈。
[Worker-1] 🎯 生成优化方向成功: 瓶颈定位：同质率0.32属强异质图，GATv2直接邻居聚合引发特征冲突；JK-Max过滤过度；Hidden=256容量见顶；50次TTA叠加Dropout致推理噪声大，Loss降而ACC卡死0.53。

调优方案：
1.架构异质化：3层GATv2引入H2GCN核心思想，强制Ego-Neighbor表征分离拼接，JK改Concat保留多跳尺度。
2.容量与归一化：Hidden升至384，全层LayerNorm稳深层梯度。
3.正则精简：Dropout=0.2，DropPath=0.05，严禁DropEdge；验证集关闭TTA单步评估，测试仅10次Dropout-TTA。
4.预处理与超参：仅Z-score，弃PCA；lr=2e-4，wd=5e-4，5%Warmup+余弦退火；CE+LabelSmoothing(0.05)，Epoch=400，Patience=40。直击异质表征瓶颈。

1. 图结构与架构：严格遵循探查结论，弃用GATv2，切换**GPR-GNN**或**MixHop**异质感知架构，显式分离Ego与Neighbor特征，混合1-3跳邻居以适配低同质率。
2. 容量与连接：保持3层，Hidden升至128。全层采用**LayerNorm**+**残差直连**，JK跳跃改用**Concat**拼接，完整保留多跳异质表征。
3. 预处理与正则：特征仅做**Z-score标准化**，关闭PCA防判别信息丢失。执行轻量正则策略，仅保留**Dropout=0.2**，**禁用DropPath/DropEdge/标签平滑**，避免叠加压制梯度导致欠拟合。

[Worker-1] 生成的训练代码：

import os
import math
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
from torch_geometric.data import Data
from torch_geometric.nn import GATv2Conv
from torch_geometric.utils import add_self_loops, coalesce
from scipy.sparse import csr_matrix
from sklearn.preprocessing import StandardScaler
from torch.optim.lr_scheduler import SequentialLR, LinearLR, CosineAnnealingLR
import warnings
warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')

# 设置随机种子保证可复现
SEED = 42
torch.manual_seed(SEED)
np.random.seed(SEED)
if torch.cuda.is_available():
    torch.cuda.manual_seed_all(SEED)
    torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = True
    torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = True

# ================= 1. 数据加载与预处理 =================
data_dir = '/data/coding/line3/dataset_b/cls_data/'
raw = np.load(os.path.join(data_dir, "B1.npz"))

adj = csr_matrix((raw["adj_data"], raw["adj_indices"], raw["adj_indptr"]), shape=tuple(raw["adj_shape"]))
features = csr_matrix((raw["attr_data"], raw["attr_indices"], raw["attr_indptr"]), shape=tuple(raw["attr_shape"]))

# 构建 edge_index：严格保留原始有向关系，仅去重并添加自环
adj_coo = adj.tocoo()
edge_index = torch.tensor(np.vstack([adj_coo.row, adj_coo.col]), dtype=torch.long)
edge_index = coalesce(edge_index)
edge_index, _ = add_self_loops(edge_index, num_nodes=adj.shape[0])

# 特征预处理：仅 Z-score 标准化
x_dense = features.toarray()
scaler = StandardScaler()
x_scaled = scaler.fit_transform(x_dense)
x = torch.tensor(x_scaled, dtype=torch.float32)

y = torch.tensor(raw["labels"], dtype=torch.long)
train_idx = raw["train_idx"]
test_idx = raw["test_idx"]

data = Data(x=x, edge_index=edge_index, y=y)

# ================= 2. 划分训练集/验证集 =================
def _reproduce_val_split(train_idx: np.ndarray) -> tuple:
    rng = np.random.RandomState(666)
    shuffled_idx = rng.permutation(train_idx)
    n_actual_train = math.ceil(len(shuffled_idx) * 0.8)
    actual_train_idx = shuffled_idx[:n_actual_train]
    val_idx = shuffled_idx[n_actual_train:]
    return actual_train_idx, val_idx

actual_train_idx, val_idx = _reproduce_val_split(train_idx)

device = torch.device('cuda' if torch.cuda.is_available() else 'cpu')
data = data.to(device)
train_idx_t = torch.tensor(actual_train_idx, dtype=torch.long, device=device)
val_idx_t = torch.tensor(val_idx, dtype=torch.long, device=device)
test_idx_t = torch.tensor(test_idx, dtype=torch.long, device=device)

# ================= 3. 模型定义 =================
class HeteroGATv2(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, in_channels, hidden_channels, out_channels, heads=4, dropout=0.2):
        super().__init__()
        self.dropout = dropout
        self.convs = nn.ModuleList()
        self.norms = nn.ModuleList()
        dim = hidden_channels * heads
        
        # 3层 GATv2，统一输出维度 dim
        self.convs.append(GATv2Conv(in_channels, hidden_channels, heads=heads, dropout=dropout, concat=True))
        self.norms.append(nn.LayerNorm(dim))
        self.convs.append(GATv2Conv(dim, hidden_channels, heads=heads, dropout=dropout, concat=True))
        self.norms.append(nn.LayerNorm(dim))
        self.convs.append(GATv2Conv(dim, hidden_channels, heads=heads, dropout=dropout, concat=True))
        self.norms.append(nn.LayerNorm(dim))
        
        # JK-Concat 分类头
        self.classifier = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Linear(dim * 3, dim),
            nn.LayerNorm(dim),
            nn.GELU(),
            nn.Dropout(dropout),
            nn.Linear(dim, out_channels)
        )

    def forward(self, x, edge_index):
        xs = []
        for i, (conv, norm) in enumerate(zip(self.convs, self.norms)):
            x = conv(x, edge_index)
            x = norm(x)
            if i < len(self.convs) - 1:
                x = F.gelu(x)
                x = F.dropout(x, p=self.dropout, training=self.training)
            xs.append(x)
        # JK-Concat 聚合多跳特征
        out = torch.cat(xs, dim=1)
        return self.classifier(out)

model = HeteroGATv2(in_channels=x.shape[1], hidden_channels=384, out_channels=8, heads=4, dropout=0.2).to(device)
optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(model.parameters(), lr=2e-4, weight_decay=5e-4)

# 5% Warmup + 余弦退火
epochs = 400
warmup_iters = int(epochs * 0.05)
warmup_scheduler = LinearLR(optimizer, start_factor=0.1, end_factor=1.0, total_iters=warmup_iters)
cosine_scheduler = CosineAnnealingLR(optimizer, T_max=epochs - warmup_iters, eta_min=1e-6)
scheduler = SequentialLR(optimizer, schedulers=[warmup_scheduler, cosine_scheduler], milestones=[warmup_iters])

criterion = nn.CrossEntropyLoss(label_smoothing=0.05)

# ================= 4. 训练配置 =================
save_path = './model_save_vb17/cls_model/worker_1/round_6'
os.makedirs(save_path, exist_ok=True)

def save_softmax_csv(idx, probs, filename, path):
    cols = ['test_idx'] + [f'class_{i}' for i in range(8)]
    df = pd.DataFrame(probs, columns=cols[1:])
    df.insert(0, 'test_idx', idx.astype(int))
    df.to_csv(os.path.join(path, filename), index=False, float_format='%.4f')

best_val_acc = 0.0
patience = 40
wait = 0

if torch.cuda.is_available():
    torch.cuda.empty_cache()

# ================= 5. 训练循环 =================
for epoch in range(1, epochs + 1):
    model.train()
    optimizer.zero_grad()
    out = model(data.x, data.edge_index)
    loss = criterion(out[train_idx_t], data.y[train_idx_t])
    loss.backward()
    optimizer.step()
    scheduler.step()

    if epoch % 10 == 0 or epoch == 1:
        model.eval()
        with torch.no_grad():
            out_eval = model(data.x, data.edge_index)
            val_pred = out_eval[val_idx_t].argmax(dim=1)
            val_acc = (val_pred == data.y[val_idx_t]).float().mean().item()

        print(f"Epoch {epoch:03d} | Val ACC: {val_acc:.4f} | Loss: {loss.item():.4f}")

        if val_acc > best_val_acc:
            best_val_acc = val_acc
            wait = 0
            torch.save(model.state_dict(), os.path.join(save_path, 'best_model.pth'))
            print(f"  -> New best model saved! Val ACC: {val_acc:.4f}")

            # 严格索引对齐保存验证集 Softmax
            val_probs = F.softmax(out_eval[val_idx_t], dim=1).cpu().numpy()
            save_softmax_csv(val_idx, val_probs, 'validation-softmax.csv', save_path)

            # 严格索引对齐保存测试集 Softmax (Eval模式)
            test_probs = F.softmax(out_eval[test_idx_t], dim=1).cpu().numpy()
            save_softmax_csv(test_idx, test_probs, 'B1-softmax.csv', save_path)

            # 保存 B1.csv
            test_pred = test_probs.argmax(axis=1)
            df_b1 = pd.DataFrame({'test_idx': test_idx.astype(int), 'label': test_pred})
            df_b1.to_csv(os.path.join(save_path, 'B1.csv'), index=False)
        else:
            wait += 1
            if wait >= patience:
                print(f"Early stopping at epoch {epoch}")
                break

# ================= 6. 测试集 TTA 增强 =================
print("Starting TTA for test set...")
model.load_state_dict(torch.load(os.path.join(save_path, 'best_model.pth'), map_location=device))
model.train()  # 开启 Dropout
tta_runs = 45
tta_probs = np.zeros((len(test_idx), 8))

with torch.no_grad():
    for i in range(tta_runs):
        out = model(data.x, data.edge_index)
        probs = F.softmax(out[test_idx_t], dim=1).cpu().numpy()
        tta_probs += probs
        if (i + 1) % 10 == 0:
            print(f"TTA run {i+1}/{tta_runs}")
tta_probs /= tta_runs

# 覆盖最终输出文件
save_softmax_csv(test_idx, tta_probs, 'B1-softmax.csv', save_path)
final_pred = tta_probs.argmax(axis=1)
df_b1 = pd.DataFrame({'test_idx': test_idx.astype(int), 'label': final_pred})
df_b1.to_csv(os.path.join(save_path, 'B1.csv'), index=False)
print("TTA completed. Final B1.csv and B1-softmax.csv saved successfully.")

[Worker-1] 🔥 执行冒烟测试...
[Worker-2] 生成的优化方向为: 瓶颈定位：同质率0.32属强异质图，GATv2邻居聚合受干扰致注意力失效；历史大隐层(256+)与多重正则叠加引发过拟合，PCA损失判别信息，ACC卡在0.54。

落地方案：
1.架构：3层GATv2引入Ego-Neighbor分离（中心与邻居特征独立变换后拼接），保留JK-Max聚合。
2.容量/归一化：hidden=128，全LayerNorm，弃BN与DropPath。
3.预处理/拓扑：仅Z-score，关闭PCA；对称归一化+强制自环，训练期禁任何拓扑扰动。
4.正则/推理：仅保留Dropout=0.2，禁DropEdge/DropNode；推理仅用20次Dropout TTA。
5.超参：CE+LabelSmoothing(0.05)，AdamW(lr=3e-4,wd=5e-4)，10轮Warmup+余弦退火，EarlyStopping(patience=30)，epochs=400。

预期：解耦异质邻居干扰，精简正则防过拟合，目标Val ACC突破0.56。

[Worker-0] 生成的训练代码：

import os
import math
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
from torch_geometric.data import Data
from torch_geometric.nn import GCNConv
from torch_geometric.utils import add_self_loops, coalesce, to_undirected
from scipy.sparse import csr_matrix
from sklearn.preprocessing import StandardScaler
from torch.optim.lr_scheduler import SequentialLR, LinearLR, CosineAnnealingLR
import warnings
warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')

# 设置随机种子保证可复现
torch.manual_seed(42)
np.random.seed(42)
if torch.cuda.is_available():
    torch.cuda.manual_seed_all(42)
    torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = True

# ================= 1. 数据加载与预处理 =================
data_dir = '/data/coding/line3/dataset_b/cls_data/'
raw = np.load(os.path.join(data_dir, "B1.npz"))

adj = csr_matrix((raw["adj_data"], raw["adj_indices"], raw["adj_indptr"]), shape=tuple(raw["adj_shape"]))
features = csr_matrix((raw["attr_data"], raw["attr_indices"], raw["attr_indptr"]), shape=tuple(raw["attr_shape"]))

# 构建 edge_index：强制对称归一化 + 自环 + 去重
adj_coo = adj.tocoo()
edge_index = torch.tensor(np.vstack([adj_coo.row, adj_coo.col]), dtype=torch.long)
edge_index = to_undirected(edge_index, num_nodes=adj.shape[0])
edge_index, _ = add_self_loops(edge_index, num_nodes=adj.shape[0])
edge_index = coalesce(edge_index)

# 特征预处理：仅 Z-score 标准化，关闭 PCA 保真
x_dense = features.toarray()
scaler = StandardScaler()
x_scaled = scaler.fit_transform(x_dense)
x = torch.tensor(x_scaled, dtype=torch.float32)

y = torch.tensor(raw["labels"], dtype=torch.long)
train_idx = raw["train_idx"]
test_idx = raw["test_idx"]

data = Data(x=x, edge_index=edge_index, y=y)

# ================= 2. 划分训练集/验证集 =================
def _reproduce_val_split(train_idx: np.ndarray) -> tuple:
    rng = np.random.RandomState(666)
    shuffled_idx = rng.permutation(train_idx)
    n_actual_train = math.ceil(len(shuffled_idx) * 0.8)
    actual_train_idx = shuffled_idx[:n_actual_train]
    val_idx = shuffled_idx[n_actual_train:]
    return actual_train_idx, val_idx

actual_train_idx, val_idx = _reproduce_val_split(train_idx)

# 设备与张量转换
device = torch.device('cuda' if torch.cuda.is_available() else 'cpu')
data = data.to(device)
train_idx_t = torch.tensor(actual_train_idx, dtype=torch.long, device=device)
val_idx_t = torch.tensor(val_idx, dtype=torch.long, device=device)
test_idx_t = torch.tensor(test_idx, dtype=torch.long, device=device)

# ================= 3. 模型定义 =================
class HeteroGNN(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, in_channels, hidden_channels, out_channels, dropout=0.2):
        super().__init__()
        self.dropout = dropout
        self.convs = nn.ModuleList()
        self.norms = nn.ModuleList()
        
        # 3层 GCN，显式分离多跳特征，适配低同质率
        self.convs.append(GCNConv(in_channels, hidden_channels, add_self_loops=True))
        self.norms.append(nn.LayerNorm(hidden_channels))
        self.convs.append(GCNConv(hidden_channels, hidden_channels, add_self_loops=True))
        self.norms.append(nn.LayerNorm(hidden_channels))
        self.convs.append(GCNConv(hidden_channels, hidden_channels, add_self_loops=True))
        self.norms.append(nn.LayerNorm(hidden_channels))
        
        # JK跳跃改用Concat拼接，完整保留多跳异质表征
        self.classifier = nn.Linear(hidden_channels * 3, out_channels)

    def forward(self, x, edge_index):
        xs = []
        for i, (conv, norm) in enumerate(zip(self.convs, self.norms)):
            h = conv(x, edge_index)
            h = norm(h)
            if i > 0:
                h = h + x  # 残差直连
            if i < len(self.convs) - 1:
                x = F.gelu(h)
                x = F.dropout(x, p=self.dropout, training=self.training)
            else:
                x = h
            xs.append(x)
        out = torch.cat(xs, dim=1)
        return self.classifier(out)

# Hidden升至128，Dropout=0.2，禁用其他正则
model = HeteroGNN(in_channels=x.shape[1], hidden_channels=128, out_channels=8, dropout=0.2).to(device)
optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(model.parameters(), lr=2e-3, weight_decay=5e-4)

# 5% Warmup + 余弦退火
epochs = 400
warmup_iters = int(epochs * 0.05)
warmup_scheduler = LinearLR(optimizer, start_factor=0.1, end_factor=1.0, total_iters=warmup_iters)
cosine_scheduler = CosineAnnealingLR(optimizer, T_max=epochs-warmup_iters, eta_min=1e-6)
scheduler = SequentialLR(optimizer, schedulers=[warmup_scheduler, cosine_scheduler], milestones=[warmup_iters])

criterion = nn.CrossEntropyLoss() # 禁用标签平滑

# ================= 4. 训练配置 =================
save_path = './model_save_vb17/cls_model/worker_0/round_4'
os.makedirs(save_path, exist_ok=True)

def save_softmax_csv(idx, probs, filename, path):
    cols = ['test_idx'] + [f'class_{i}' for i in range(8)]
    df = pd.DataFrame(probs, columns=cols[1:])
    df.insert(0, 'test_idx', idx.astype(int))
    df.to_csv(os.path.join(path, filename), index=False, float_format='%.4f')

best_val_acc = 0.0
patience = 50
wait = 0

if torch.cuda.is_available():
    torch.cuda.empty_cache()

# ================= 5. 训练循环 =================
for epoch in range(1, epochs + 1):
    model.train()
    optimizer.zero_grad()
    out = model(data.x, data.edge_index)
    loss = criterion(out[train_idx_t], data.y[train_idx_t])
    loss.backward()
    optimizer.step()
    scheduler.step()

    if epoch % 10 == 0 or epoch == 1:
        model.eval()
        with torch.no_grad():
            out_eval = model(data.x, data.edge_index)
            val_pred = out_eval[val_idx_t].argmax(dim=1)
            val_acc = (val_pred == data.y[val_idx_t]).float().mean().item()

        print(f"Epoch {epoch:03d} | Val ACC: {val_acc:.4f} | Loss: {loss.item():.4f}")

        if val_acc > best_val_acc:
            best_val_acc = val_acc
            wait = 0
            torch.save(model.state_dict(), os.path.join(save_path, 'best_model.pth'))
            print(f"  -> New best model saved! Val ACC: {val_acc:.4f}")

            # 严格索引对齐保存验证集 Softmax
            val_probs = F.softmax(out_eval[val_idx_t], dim=1).cpu().numpy()
            save_softmax_csv(val_idx, val_probs, 'validation-softmax.csv', save_path)

            # 严格索引对齐保存测试集 Softmax (Eval模式)
            test_probs = F.softmax(out_eval[test_idx_t], dim=1).cpu().numpy()
            save_softmax_csv(test_idx, test_probs, 'B1-softmax.csv', save_path)

            # 保存 B1.csv
            test_pred = test_probs.argmax(axis=1)
            df_b1 = pd.DataFrame({'test_idx': test_idx.astype(int), 'label': test_pred})
            df_b1.to_csv(os.path.join(save_path, 'B1.csv'), index=False)
        else:
            wait += 1
            if wait >= patience:
                print(f"Early stopping at epoch {epoch}")
                break

# ================= 6. 测试集 TTA 增强 =================
print("Starting TTA for test set...")
model.load_state_dict(torch.load(os.path.join(save_path, 'best_model.pth'), map_location=device))
model.train()  # 开启 Dropout
tta_runs = 50
tta_probs = np.zeros((len(test_idx), 8))

with torch.no_grad():
    for _ in range(tta_runs):
        out = model(data.x, data.edge_index)
        probs = F.softmax(out[test_idx_t], dim=1).cpu().numpy()
        tta_probs += probs
tta_probs /= tta_runs

# 覆盖最终输出文件
save_softmax_csv(test_idx, tta_probs, 'B1-softmax.csv', save_path)
final_pred = tta_probs.argmax(axis=1)
df_b1 = pd.DataFrame({'test_idx': test_idx.astype(int), 'label': final_pred})
df_b1.to_csv(os.path.join(save_path, 'B1.csv'), index=False)
print("TTA completed. Final B1.csv and B1-softmax.csv saved successfully.")

[Worker-2] 生成的训练代码：

import os
import math
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
from torch_geometric.data import Data
from torch_geometric.nn import GATv2Conv
from torch_geometric.utils import coalesce
from scipy.sparse import csr_matrix
from sklearn.preprocessing import StandardScaler
from torch.optim.lr_scheduler import SequentialLR, LinearLR, CosineAnnealingLR
import warnings
warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')

# 设置随机种子保证可复现
torch.manual_seed(42)
np.random.seed(42)
if torch.cuda.is_available():
    torch.cuda.manual_seed_all(42)
    torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = True

# ================= 1. 数据加载与预处理 =================
data_dir = '/data/coding/line3/dataset_b/cls_data/'
raw = np.load(os.path.join(data_dir, "B1.npz"))

adj = csr_matrix((raw["adj_data"], raw["adj_indices"], raw["adj_indptr"]), shape=tuple(raw["adj_shape"]))
features = csr_matrix((raw["attr_data"], raw["attr_indices"], raw["attr_indptr"]), shape=tuple(raw["attr_shape"]))

# 严格按原样使用邻接关系，不假设无向/对称/无自环
adj_coo = adj.tocoo()
edge_index = torch.tensor(np.vstack([adj_coo.row, adj_coo.col]), dtype=torch.long)
edge_index = coalesce(edge_index, num_nodes=adj.shape[0])

# 特征预处理：仅 Z-score 标准化
x_dense = features.toarray()
scaler = StandardScaler()
x = torch.tensor(scaler.fit_transform(x_dense), dtype=torch.float32)

y = torch.tensor(raw["labels"], dtype=torch.long)
train_idx = raw["train_idx"]
test_idx = raw["test_idx"]

data = Data(x=x, edge_index=edge_index, y=y)

# ================= 2. 划分训练集/验证集 =================
def _reproduce_val_split(train_idx: np.ndarray) -> tuple:
    rng = np.random.RandomState(666)
    shuffled_idx = rng.permutation(train_idx)
    n_actual_train = math.ceil(len(shuffled_idx) * 0.8)
    actual_train_idx = shuffled_idx[:n_actual_train]
    val_idx = shuffled_idx[n_actual_train:]
    return actual_train_idx, val_idx

actual_train_idx, val_idx = _reproduce_val_split(train_idx)

device = torch.device('cuda' if torch.cuda.is_available() else 'cpu')
data = data.to(device)
train_idx_t = torch.tensor(actual_train_idx, dtype=torch.long, device=device)
val_idx_t = torch.tensor(val_idx, dtype=torch.long, device=device)
test_idx_t = torch.tensor(test_idx, dtype=torch.long, device=device)

# ================= 3. 模型定义 =================
class GATv2JKModel(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, in_channels, hidden_channels, out_channels, heads=4, dropout=0.2):
        super().__init__()
        self.dropout = dropout
        self.convs = nn.ModuleList()
        self.norms = nn.ModuleList()
        dim = hidden_channels * heads
        
        self.convs.append(GATv2Conv(in_channels, hidden_channels, heads=heads, dropout=dropout, concat=True))
        self.norms.append(nn.LayerNorm(dim))
        self.convs.append(GATv2Conv(dim, hidden_channels, heads=heads, dropout=dropout, concat=True))
        self.norms.append(nn.LayerNorm(dim))
        self.convs.append(GATv2Conv(dim, hidden_channels, heads=heads, dropout=dropout, concat=True))
        self.norms.append(nn.LayerNorm(dim))
        
        self.classifier = nn.Linear(dim, out_channels)

    def forward(self, x, edge_index):
        xs = []
        for i, (conv, norm) in enumerate(zip(self.convs, self.norms)):
            x = conv(x, edge_index)
            x = norm(x)
            if i < len(self.convs) - 1:
                x = F.gelu(x)
                x = F.dropout(x, p=self.dropout, training=self.training)
            xs.append(x)
        out = torch.stack(xs, dim=0).max(dim=0)[0]
        return self.classifier(out)

model = GATv2JKModel(in_channels=x.shape[1], hidden_channels=128, out_channels=8, heads=4, dropout=0.2).to(device)
optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(model.parameters(), lr=3e-4, weight_decay=5e-4)

epochs = 400
warmup_scheduler = LinearLR(optimizer, start_factor=0.1, end_factor=1.0, total_iters=10)
cosine_scheduler = CosineAnnealingLR(optimizer, T_max=epochs-10, eta_min=1e-6)
scheduler = SequentialLR(optimizer, schedulers=[warmup_scheduler, cosine_scheduler], milestones=[10])

criterion = nn.CrossEntropyLoss(label_smoothing=0.05)

# ================= 4. 训练配置 =================
save_path = './model_save_vb17/cls_model/worker_2/round_6'
os.makedirs(save_path, exist_ok=True)

def save_softmax_csv(idx, probs, filename, path):
    cols = ['test_idx'] + [f'class_{i}' for i in range(8)]
    df = pd.DataFrame(probs, columns=cols[1:])
    df.insert(0, 'test_idx', idx.astype(int))
    df.to_csv(os.path.join(path, filename), index=False, float_format='%.4f')

best_val_acc = 0.0
patience = 40
wait = 0

if torch.cuda.is_available():
    torch.cuda.empty_cache()

# ================= 5. 训练循环 =================
for epoch in range(1, epochs + 1):
    model.train()
    optimizer.zero_grad()
    out = model(data.x, data.edge_index)
    loss = criterion(out[train_idx_t], data.y[train_idx_t])
    loss.backward()
    optimizer.step()
    scheduler.step()

    if epoch % 10 == 0 or epoch == 1:
        model.eval()
        with torch.no_grad():
            out_eval = model(data.x, data.edge_index)
            val_pred = out_eval[val_idx_t].argmax(dim=1)
            val_acc = (val_pred == data.y[val_idx_t]).float().mean().item()

        print(f"Epoch {epoch:03d} | Val ACC: {val_acc:.4f} | Loss: {loss.item():.4f}")

        if val_acc > best_val_acc:
            best_val_acc = val_acc
            wait = 0
            torch.save(model.state_dict(), os.path.join(save_path, 'best_model.pth'))
            print(f"  -> New best model saved! Val ACC: {val_acc:.4f}")

            # 严格索引对齐保存验证集 Softmax
            val_probs = F.softmax(out_eval[val_idx_t], dim=1).cpu().numpy()
            save_softmax_csv(val_idx, val_probs, 'validation-softmax.csv', save_path)

            # 严格索引对齐保存测试集 Softmax (Eval模式)
            test_probs = F.softmax(out_eval[test_idx_t], dim=1).cpu().numpy()
            save_softmax_csv(test_idx, test_probs, 'B1-softmax.csv', save_path)

            # 保存 B1.csv
            test_pred = test_probs.argmax(axis=1)
            df_b1 = pd.DataFrame({'test_idx': test_idx.astype(int), 'label': test_pred})
            df_b1.to_csv(os.path.join(save_path, 'B1.csv'), index=False)
        else:
            wait += 1
            if wait >= patience:
                print(f"Early stopping at epoch {epoch}")
                break

# ================= 6. 测试集 TTA 增强 =================
print("Starting TTA for test set...")
model.load_state_dict(torch.load(os.path.join(save_path, 'best_model.pth'), map_location=device))
model.train()  # 开启 Dropout
tta_runs = 50
tta_probs = np.zeros((len(test_idx), 8))

with torch.no_grad():
    for _ in range(tta_runs):
        out = model(data.x, data.edge_index)
        probs = F.softmax(out[test_idx_t], dim=1).cpu().numpy()
        tta_probs += probs
tta_probs /= tta_runs

# 覆盖最终输出文件
save_softmax_csv(test_idx, tta_probs, 'B1-softmax.csv', save_path)
final_pred = tta_probs.argmax(axis=1)
df_b1 = pd.DataFrame({'test_idx': test_idx.astype(int), 'label': final_pred})
df_b1.to_csv(os.path.join(save_path, 'B1.csv'), index=False)
print("TTA completed. Final B1.csv and B1-softmax.csv saved successfully.")

   worker_2/round_0: 0.4886
   worker_2/round_1: 0.4828
   worker_2/round_2: 0.5065
   worker_2/round_3: 0.4943
   worker_2/round_4: 0.4975
   worker_2/round_5: 0.4959
   => 集成 Val ACC: 0.5400 (选中 6 个模型)

   - test_idx 范围: [9, 7648]
   - label 分布: {0: 55, 1: 403, 2: 108, 3: 158, 4: 139, 5: 151, 6: 489, 7: 27}
[Worker-1] 生成的优化方向为: 瓶颈定位：同质率0.32属典型异质图，纯GATv2未解耦中心-邻居特征导致注意力混叠；R15(hidden=64)达0.5466为当前最优，但容量受限；PCA降维与多重正则叠加引发欠拟合/过拟合震荡；50次TTA引入概率噪声。

调优方案：
1. 架构与异质适配：以R15为基线，3层GATv2。每层显式分离Ego与Neighbor特征后Concat过MLP，适配低同质率；保留残差直连与JK-Max跳跃聚合，打破多跳表征瓶颈。
2. 容量与归一化：hidden_dim调至128（平衡R15低容与R9高容），heads=4；全层采用LayerNorm（弃用BN），稳定异质图注意力分布。
3. 预处理与正则：仅保留Z-score标准化，彻底移除PCA/SVD；正则降级为Dropout=0.2+DropPath=0.05，禁用DropEdge/Node；推理仅用20次Dropout TTA。
4. 训练超参：对称归一化+强制自环；损失用CE+LabelSmoothing(0.05)，弃用类别加权；AdamW(lr=3e-4, wd=5e-4)，10轮Warmup+余弦退火，Epochs=450，EarlyStopping(patience=50)。

预期：异质特征解耦突破混叠天花板，轻量正则与精准容量压制平台期震荡，目标Val ACC冲击0.58+。

预期：结构解耦适配低同质率，精简容量与正则释放泛化空间，目标Val ACC突破0.56。基于R15最优基线微调，风险可控。

[Worker-0] 🔄 进入全量生成阶段
[Worker-1] 生成的训练代码：

import os
import math
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
from torch_geometric.data import Data
from torch_geometric.nn import GATv2Conv
from torch_geometric.utils import add_self_loops, coalesce, to_undirected
from scipy.sparse import csr_matrix
from sklearn.preprocessing import StandardScaler
from torch.optim.lr_scheduler import SequentialLR, LinearLR, CosineAnnealingLR
import warnings
warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')

# 设置随机种子保证可复现
torch.manual_seed(42)
np.random.seed(42)
if torch.cuda.is_available():
    torch.cuda.manual_seed_all(42)
    torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = True

# ================= 1. 数据加载与预处理 =================
data_dir = '/data/coding/line3/dataset_b/cls_data/'
raw = np.load(os.path.join(data_dir, "B1.npz"))

adj = csr_matrix((raw["adj_data"], raw["adj_indices"], raw["adj_indptr"]), shape=tuple(raw["adj_shape"]))
features = csr_matrix((raw["attr_data"], raw["attr_indices"], raw["attr_indptr"]), shape=tuple(raw["attr_shape"]))

# 构建 edge_index：强制对称化 + 自环 + 去重
adj_coo = adj.tocoo()
edge_index = torch.tensor(np.vstack([adj_coo.row, adj_coo.col]), dtype=torch.long)
edge_index = to_undirected(edge_index, num_nodes=adj.shape[0])
edge_index, _ = add_self_loops(edge_index, num_nodes=adj.shape[0])
edge_index = coalesce(edge_index)

# 特征预处理：仅 Z-score 标准化
x_dense = features.toarray()
scaler = StandardScaler()
x_scaled = scaler.fit_transform(x_dense)
x = torch.tensor(x_scaled, dtype=torch.float32)

y = torch.tensor(raw["labels"], dtype=torch.long)
train_idx = raw["train_idx"]
test_idx = raw["test_idx"]

data = Data(x=x, edge_index=edge_index, y=y)

# ================= 2. 划分训练集/验证集 =================
def _reproduce_val_split(train_idx: np.ndarray) -> tuple:
    rng = np.random.RandomState(666)
    shuffled_idx = rng.permutation(train_idx)
    n_actual_train = math.ceil(len(shuffled_idx) * 0.8)
    actual_train_idx = shuffled_idx[:n_actual_train]
    val_idx = shuffled_idx[n_actual_train:]
    return actual_train_idx, val_idx

actual_train_idx, val_idx = _reproduce_val_split(train_idx)

# 设备与张量转换
device = torch.device('cuda' if torch.cuda.is_available() else 'cpu')
data = data.to(device)
train_idx_t = torch.tensor(actual_train_idx, dtype=torch.long, device=device)
val_idx_t = torch.tensor(val_idx, dtype=torch.long, device=device)
test_idx_t = torch.tensor(test_idx, dtype=torch.long, device=device)

# ================= 3. 模型定义 =================
class GATv2JKModel(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, in_channels, hidden_channels, out_channels, heads=4, dropout=0.2):
        super().__init__()
        self.dropout = dropout
        self.convs = nn.ModuleList()
        self.norms = nn.ModuleList()
        dim = hidden_channels * heads
        
        # 3层 GATv2，统一输出维度 dim
        for i in range(3):
            in_dim = in_channels if i == 0 else dim
            self.convs.append(GATv2Conv(in_dim, hidden_channels, heads=heads, dropout=dropout, concat=True))
            self.norms.append(nn.LayerNorm(dim))
            
        self.classifier = nn.Linear(dim, out_channels)

    def forward(self, x, edge_index):
        xs = []
        for i, (conv, norm) in enumerate(zip(self.convs, self.norms)):
            x = conv(x, edge_index)
            x = norm(x)
            if i < len(self.convs) - 1:
                x = F.gelu(x)
                x = F.dropout(x, p=self.dropout, training=self.training)
            xs.append(x)
        # JKNet (Max) 聚合多跳特征
        out = torch.stack(xs, dim=0).max(dim=0)[0]
        return self.classifier(out)

# 调优参数：hidden=128, heads=4, dropout=0.2
model = GATv2JKModel(in_channels=x.shape[1], hidden_channels=128, out_channels=8, heads=4, dropout=0.2).to(device)
optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(model.parameters(), lr=3e-4, weight_decay=5e-4)

# 10轮 Warmup + 余弦退火
epochs = 450
warmup_scheduler = LinearLR(optimizer, start_factor=0.1, end_factor=1.0, total_iters=10)
cosine_scheduler = CosineAnnealingLR(optimizer, T_max=epochs-10, eta_min=1e-6)
scheduler = SequentialLR(optimizer, schedulers=[warmup_scheduler, cosine_scheduler], milestones=[10])

criterion = nn.CrossEntropyLoss(label_smoothing=0.05)

# ================= 4. 训练配置 =================
save_path = './model_save_vb17/cls_model/worker_1/round_7'
os.makedirs(save_path, exist_ok=True)

def save_softmax_csv(idx, probs, filename, path):
    cols = ['test_idx'] + [f'class_{i}' for i in range(8)]
    df = pd.DataFrame(probs, columns=cols[1:])
    df.insert(0, 'test_idx', idx.astype(int))
    df.to_csv(os.path.join(path, filename), index=False, float_format='%.4f')

best_val_acc = 0.0
patience = 50
wait = 0

if torch.cuda.is_available():
    torch.cuda.empty_cache()

# ================= 5. 训练循环 =================
for epoch in range(1, epochs + 1):
    model.train()
    optimizer.zero_grad()
    out = model(data.x, data.edge_index)
    loss = criterion(out[train_idx_t], data.y[train_idx_t])
    loss.backward()
    optimizer.step()
    scheduler.step()

    if epoch % 10 == 0 or epoch == 1:
        model.eval()
        with torch.no_grad():
            out_eval = model(data.x, data.edge_index)
            val_pred = out_eval[val_idx_t].argmax(dim=1)
            val_acc = (val_pred == data.y[val_idx_t]).float().mean().item()

        print(f"Epoch {epoch:03d} | Val ACC: {val_acc:.4f} | Loss: {loss.item():.4f}")

        if val_acc > best_val_acc:
            best_val_acc = val_acc
            wait = 0
            torch.save(model.state_dict(), os.path.join(save_path, 'best_model.pth'))
            print(f"  -> New best model saved! Val ACC: {val_acc:.4f}")

            # 严格索引对齐保存验证集 Softmax
            val_probs = F.softmax(out_eval[val_idx_t], dim=1).cpu().numpy()
            save_softmax_csv(val_idx, val_probs, 'validation-softmax.csv', save_path)

            # 严格索引对齐保存测试集 Softmax (Eval模式)
            test_probs = F.softmax(out_eval[test_idx_t], dim=1).cpu().numpy()
            save_softmax_csv(test_idx, test_probs, 'B1-softmax.csv', save_path)

            # 保存 B1.csv
            test_pred = test_probs.argmax(axis=1)
            df_b1 = pd.DataFrame({'test_idx': test_idx.astype(int), 'label': test_pred})
            df_b1.to_csv(os.path.join(save_path, 'B1.csv'), index=False)
        else:
            wait += 1
            if wait >= patience:
                print(f"Early stopping at epoch {epoch}")
                break

# ================= 6. 测试集 TTA 增强 =================
print("Starting TTA for test set...")
model.load_state_dict(torch.load(os.path.join(save_path, 'best_model.pth'), map_location=device))
model.train()  # 开启 Dropout 进行 TTA
tta_runs = 50
tta_probs = np.zeros((len(test_idx), 8))

with torch.no_grad():
    for _ in range(tta_runs):
        out = model(data.x, data.edge_index)
        probs = F.softmax(out[test_idx_t], dim=1).cpu().numpy()
        tta_probs += probs
tta_probs /= tta_runs

# 覆盖最终输出文件
save_softmax_csv(test_idx, tta_probs, 'B1-softmax.csv', save_path)
final_pred = tta_probs.argmax(axis=1)
df_b1 = pd.DataFrame({'test_idx': test_idx.astype(int), 'label': final_pred})
df_b1.to_csv(os.path.join(save_path, 'B1.csv'), index=False)
print("TTA completed. Final B1.csv and B1-softmax.csv saved successfully.")

[Worker-0] 生成的训练代码：

import os
import math
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import torch.nn.functional as F
from torch_geometric.data import Data
from torch_geometric.nn import GATv2Conv
from torch_geometric.utils import coalesce
from scipy.sparse import csr_matrix
from sklearn.preprocessing import StandardScaler
from torch.optim.lr_scheduler import SequentialLR, LinearLR, CosineAnnealingLR
import warnings
warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')

# 设置随机种子保证可复现
torch.manual_seed(42)
np.random.seed(42)
if torch.cuda.is_available():
    torch.cuda.manual_seed_all(42)
    torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = True

# ================= 1. 数据加载与预处理 =================
data_dir = '/data/coding/line3/dataset_b/cls_data/'
raw = np.load(os.path.join(data_dir, "B1.npz"))

adj = csr_matrix((raw["adj_data"], raw["adj_indices"], raw["adj_indptr"]), shape=tuple(raw["adj_shape"]))
features = csr_matrix((raw["attr_data"], raw["attr_indices"], raw["attr_indptr"]), shape=tuple(raw["attr_shape"]))

# 构建 edge_index：严格按原样使用，不额外假设无向/对称/无自环，仅去重
adj_coo = adj.tocoo()
edge_index = torch.tensor(np.vstack([adj_coo.row, adj_coo.col]), dtype=torch.long)
edge_index = coalesce(edge_index, num_nodes=adj.shape[0])

# 特征预处理：仅 Z-score 标准化，关闭 PCA 保真
x_dense = features.toarray()
scaler = StandardScaler()
x_scaled = scaler.fit_transform(x_dense)
x = torch.tensor(x_scaled, dtype=torch.float32)

y = torch.tensor(raw["labels"], dtype=torch.long)
train_idx = raw["train_idx"]
test_idx = raw["test_idx"]

data = Data(x=x, edge_index=edge_index, y=y)

# ================= 2. 划分训练集/验证集 =================
def _reproduce_val_split(train_idx: np.ndarray) -> tuple:
    rng = np.random.RandomState(666)
    shuffled_idx = rng.permutation(train_idx)
    n_actual_train = math.ceil(len(shuffled_idx) * 0.8)
    actual_train_idx = shuffled_idx[:n_actual_train]
    val_idx = shuffled_idx[n_actual_train:]
    return actual_train_idx, val_idx

actual_train_idx, val_idx = _reproduce_val_split(train_idx)

device = torch.device('cuda' if torch.cuda.is_available() else 'cpu')
data = data.to(device)
train_idx_t = torch.tensor(actual_train_idx, dtype=torch.long, device=device)
val_idx_t = torch.tensor(val_idx, dtype=torch.long, device=device)
test_idx_t = torch.tensor(test_idx, dtype=torch.long, device=device)

# ================= 3. 模型定义 =================
class GATv2JKModel(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, in_channels, hidden_channels, out_channels, heads=4, dropout=0.2):
        super().__init__()
        self.dropout = dropout
        self.convs = nn.ModuleList()
        self.norms = nn.ModuleList()
        dim = hidden_channels * heads
        
        # 3层 GATv2，统一输出维度 dim
        self.convs.append(GATv2Conv(in_channels, hidden_channels, heads=heads, dropout=dropout, concat=True))
        self.norms.append(nn.LayerNorm(dim))
        self.convs.append(GATv2Conv(dim, hidden_channels, heads=heads, dropout=dropout, concat=True))
        self.norms.append(nn.LayerNorm(dim))
        self.convs.append(GATv2Conv(dim, hidden_channels, heads=heads, dropout=dropout, concat=True))
        self.norms.append(nn.LayerNorm(dim))
        
        self.classifier = nn.Linear(dim, out_channels)

    def forward(self, x, edge_index):
        xs = []
        for i, (conv, norm) in enumerate(zip(self.convs, self.norms)):
            x = conv(x, edge_index)
            x = norm(x)
            if i < len(self.convs) - 1:
                x = F.gelu(x)
                x = F.dropout(x, p=self.dropout, training=self.training)
            xs.append(x)
        # JKNet (Max) 聚合多跳特征
        out = torch.stack(xs, dim=0).max(dim=0)[0]
        return self.classifier(out)

model = GATv2JKModel(in_channels=x.shape[1], hidden_channels=64, out_channels=8, heads=4, dropout=0.2).to(device)
optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(model.parameters(), lr=3e-4, weight_decay=5e-4)

# 10轮 Warmup + 余弦退火
epochs = 400
warmup_scheduler = LinearLR(optimizer, start_factor=0.1, end_factor=1.0, total_iters=10)
cosine_scheduler = CosineAnnealingLR(optimizer, T_max=epochs-10, eta_min=1e-6)
scheduler = SequentialLR(optimizer, schedulers=[warmup_scheduler, cosine_scheduler], milestones=[10])

criterion = nn.CrossEntropyLoss(label_smoothing=0.05)

# ================= 4. 训练配置 =================
save_path = './model_save_vb17/cls_model/worker_0/round_5'
os.makedirs(save_path, exist_ok=True)

def save_softmax_csv(idx, probs, filename, path):
    cols = ['test_idx'] + [f'class_{i}' for i in range(8)]
    df = pd.DataFrame(probs, columns=cols[1:])
    df.insert(0, 'test_idx', idx.astype(int))
    df.to_csv(os.path.join(path, filename), index=False, float_format='%.4f')

best_val_acc = 0.0
patience = 40
wait = 0

if torch.cuda.is_available():
    torch.cuda.empty_cache()

# ================= 5. 训练循环 =================
for epoch in range(1, epochs + 1):
    model.train()
    optimizer.zero_grad()
    out = model(data.x, data.edge_index)
    loss = criterion(out[train_idx_t], data.y[train_idx_t])
    loss.backward()
    optimizer.step()
    scheduler.step()

    if epoch % 10 == 0 or epoch == 1:
        model.eval()
        with torch.no_grad():
            out_eval = model(data.x, data.edge_index)
            val_pred = out_eval[val_idx_t].argmax(dim=1)
            val_acc = (val_pred == data.y[val_idx_t]).float().mean().item()

        print(f"Epoch {epoch:03d} | Val ACC: {val_acc:.4f} | Loss: {loss.item():.4f}")

        if val_acc > best_val_acc:
            best_val_acc = val_acc
            wait = 0
            torch.save(model.state_dict(), os.path.join(save_path, 'best_model.pth'))
            print(f"  -> New best model saved! Val ACC: {val_acc:.4f}")

            # 严格索引对齐保存验证集 Softmax
            val_probs = F.softmax(out_eval[val_idx], dim=1).cpu().numpy()
            save_softmax_csv(val_idx, val_probs, 'validation-softmax.csv', save_path)

            # 严格索引对齐保存测试集 Softmax (Eval模式)
            test_probs = F.softmax(out_eval[test_idx], dim=1).cpu().numpy()
            save_softmax_csv(test_idx, test_probs, 'B1-softmax.csv', save_path)

            # 保存 B1.csv
            test_pred = test_probs.argmax(axis=1)
            df_b1 = pd.DataFrame({'test_idx': test_idx.astype(int), 'label': test_pred})
            df_b1.to_csv(os.path.join(save_path, 'B1.csv'), index=False)
        else:
            wait += 1
            if wait >= patience:
                print(f"Early stopping at epoch {epoch}")
                break

# ================= 6. 测试集 TTA 增强 =================
print("Starting TTA for test set...")
model.load_state_dict(torch.load(os.path.join(save_path, 'best_model.pth'), map_location=device))
model.train()  # 开启 Dropout
tta_runs = 50
tta_probs = np.zeros((len(test_idx), 8))

with torch.no_grad():
    for _ in range(tta_runs):
        out = model(data.x, data.edge_index)
        probs = F.softmax(out[test_idx], dim=1).cpu().numpy()
        tta_probs += probs
tta_probs /= tta_runs

# 覆盖最终输出文件
save_softmax_csv(test_idx, tta_probs, 'B1-softmax.csv', save_path)
final_pred = tta_probs.argmax(axis=1)
df_b1 = pd.DataFrame({'test_idx': test_idx.astype(int), 'label': final_pred})
df_b1.to_csv(os.path.join(save_path, 'B1.csv'), index=False)
print("TTA completed. Final B1.csv and B1-softmax.csv saved successfully.")

# 特征预处理：仅 Z-score 标准化 -> edge_index = coalesce(edge_index, num_nodes=adj.shape[0])
# 强制自环 + 对称归一化
from torch_geometric.utils import add_self_loops, degree
edge_index, _ = add_self_loops(edge_index, num_nodes=adj.shape[0])
row, col = edge_index
deg = degree(row, num_nodes=adj.shape[0], dtype=torch.float32)
deg_inv_sqrt = deg.pow(-0.5)
deg_inv_sqrt[deg_inv_sqrt == float('inf')] = 0
edge_weight = deg_inv_sqrt[row] * deg_inv_sqrt[col]

# 特征预处理：仅 Z-score 标准化

    def forward(self, x, edge_index):
        xs = []
        for i, (conv, norm) in enumerate(zip(self.convs, self.norms)):
            x = conv(x, edge_index)
            x = norm(x)
            if i < len(self.convs) - 1:

        return self.classifier(out) -> class GATv2JKModel(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, in_channels, hidden_channels, out_channels, heads=4, dropout=0.2):
        super().__init__()
        self.dropout = dropout
        self.convs = nn.ModuleList()
        self.norms = nn.ModuleList()
        self.center_lins = nn.ModuleList()
        self.fuse_lins = nn.ModuleList()
        dim = hidden_channels * heads

        self.center_lins.append(nn.Linear(in_channels, dim))
        self.convs.append(GATv2Conv(in_channels, hidden_channels, heads=heads, dropout=dropout, concat=True, add_self_loops=False))
        self.norms.append(nn.LayerNorm(dim * 2))
        self.fuse_lins.append(nn.Linear(dim * 2, dim))

        for _ in range(2):
            self.center_lins.append(nn.Linear(dim, dim))
            self.convs.append(GATv2Conv(dim, hidden_channels, heads=heads, dropout=dropout, concat=True, add_self_loops=False))
            self.norms.append(nn.LayerNorm(dim * 2))
            self.fuse_lins.append(nn.Linear(dim * 2, dim))

        self.classifier = nn.Linear(dim, out_channels)

    def forward(self, x, edge_index, edge_weight=None):
        xs = []
        edge_attr = edge_weight.unsqueeze(-1) if edge_weight is not None else None
        for i, (conv, norm, center_lin, fuse_lin) in enumerate(zip(self.convs, self.norms, self.center_lins, self.fuse_lins)):
            x_center = center_lin(x)
            x_neigh = conv(x, edge_index, edge_attr=edge_attr)
            x = torch.cat([x_center, x_neigh], dim=-1)
            x = norm(x)
            x = fuse_lin(x)
            if i < len(self.convs) - 1:
                x = F.gelu(x)
                x = F.dropout(x, p=self.dropout, training=self.training)
            xs.append(x)
        out = torch.stack(xs, dim=0).max(dim=0)[0]
        return self.classifier(out)

    # 手动 Warmup + 余弦退火
    if epoch <= 10:
        lr = 3e-4 * (epoch / 10)
    else:
        lr = 1e-6 + 0.5 * (3e-4 - 1e-6) * (1 + math.cos(math.pi * (epoch - 10) / (epochs - 10)))
    for param_group in optimizer.param_groups:
        param_group['lr'] = lr

    model.train()
    optimizer.zero_grad()
    out = model(data.x, data.edge_index, data.edge_weight)
    loss = criterion(out[train_idx_t], data.y[train_idx_t])
    loss.backward()
    optimizer.step()
